# Daily video prompt pack — 02/11/2026 · Dragon day (ថ្ងៃឆ្នាំរោង)

Lucky signs: Dragon, Rat, Monkey · Careful: Dog
Day element: Yang Metal ✨ (Geng-chen day; ធាតុលោហៈ) · every action scene today carries Metal magic: bright silver-white sparkles, shimmering blades of light and softly chiming silver stars.
Format: vertical 9:16 (YouTube Shorts, Facebook Reels, TikTok), about 45 seconds: 4 Veo scenes of about 8 seconds, then a lucky-numbers scene of about 10–12 seconds.
Two versions: make the pictures once, then record a Khmer voice-over and an English voice-over (two videos, or one video with both subtitle tracks).
Ratings, lucky signs and lucky numbers come from the site's fixed rules and match the website's daily fortune for this date.
Voice-over wording: standard script (this pack was made ahead; the site's own daily wording appears the day before).

## Today's horoscope – all 12 signs
Each sign's prediction for 02/11/2026: general luck, love, work, money and advice, in English and Khmer, ready to copy into your Facebook, TikTok, Telegram and YouTube posts. The videos speak the same predictions: the 60-second video (the Dragon's luck and love), the 180-second video (love, work and money), the long videos (every sign) and the full-fortune video. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs, as in the videos.
Wording: the website's standard lines for each sign's rating (this date's written fortune is published on the website the day before; open the Studio at mybirthsign.com/studio/ then to use it).

### Khmer
🐀 ឆ្នាំជូត – ថ្ងៃល្អ
ទូទៅ៖ ថ្ងៃនេះ ល្អសម្រាប់ជួបមនុស្ស និងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងល្អៗ។ សូមរក្សាស្នាមញញឹម។
ស្នេហា៖ សូមចំណាយពេលបន្តិចជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ។ គ្រាន់តែស្ដាប់ ក៏គ្រប់គ្រាន់ហើយ។
ការងារ៖ ការងារជាក្រុម ដំណើរការល្អ។ សូមតាមដានកិច្ចការ ឬការកុម្ម៉ង់ដែលនៅសល់ថ្ងៃនេះ។
លុយកាក់៖ ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់ពិនិត្យថវិកា ហើយសន្សំទុកបន្តិច។
ដំបូន្មាន៖ សូមធ្វើការជាមួយមិត្តភក្តិថ្ងៃនេះ ការខិតខំរួមគ្នា នាំមកនូវលទ្ធផលល្អជាង។

One hero post for the day's luckiest animal: a picture, a short video and a caption. Lucky color gold (មាស) · best time 15:00–17:00 · lucky numbers 1 · 75 · 147 · 1937 · 76106 (for fun only).

### Picture prompt (square 1:1)
Create a photorealistic hero image of @Dragon (the Dragon guardian with their dragon), the Dragon of the Chinese zodiac, as a majestic celestial guardian from a high-budget fantasy epic. The dragon symbolizes power, good fortune and a noble spirit: a majestic, proud and noble expression and posture that show this, mouth calm and closed. Realistic anatomy and textures, engraved gold ornaments with physically real metallic reflections. It stands on a high terrace of a temple tower inspired by Angkor Wat, with carved apsara reliefs behind it and the moonlit jungle far below, under an enormous luminous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky; moonbeams fall on the dragon, golden particles and flowing ribbons of liquid-gold light swirl around it. Cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light, volumetric mist, cinematic depth of field, ultra-detailed. Square 1:1, full body visible, three-quarter view, centered, with space around it. No text, no letters, no watermark, no logo, no cartoon or plastic look, no cropped body parts.

Today's luckiest zodiac animal, the Dragon, in 80 seconds. For viewers who follow Chinese zodiac signs, especially people born in the Year of the Dragon, scrolling Facebook Reels and TikTok.

## 180-second video – YouTube
A 180-second video about the Dragon, today's luckiest animal: 18 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add Chanda and the saved character @Dragon as ingredients. Chanda opens the video on camera with the hook and narrates the rest off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 18.

### Topic and audience
A three-minute guide to the Dragon's Great Day: meaning, character, love, work, money and lucky details. For people born in the Year of the Dragon and zodiac fans on YouTube Shorts.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Daily fortune
   English: Born in the Year of the Dragon? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរោងមែនទេ? នេះគឺពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ ព្រមទាំងការរំលឹកមួយផង។
2. Curiosity
   English: Out of all twelve zodiac signs, only one has a Great Day today. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានតែមួយគត់ ដែលមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំងនៅថ្ងៃនេះ។ តើជារាសីរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
3. Personality
   English: If you were born in the Year of the Dragon, people may describe you as confident, charismatic, and ambitious. Today, that side of you shines.
   Khmer: បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរោង មនុស្សជុំវិញខ្លួន ប្រហែលជាស្គាល់អ្នកថា ជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ទាក់ទាញចំណាប់អារម្មណ៍ និងមានមហិច្ឆតា។ ថ្ងៃនេះ ចំណុចល្អនោះកំពុងភ្លឺ។
4. Challenge
   English: Can you guess today's luckiest zodiac animal? Three clues: confident, charismatic, and ambitious.
   Khmer: ទាយមើល៖ តើឆ្នាំណា មានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះ? តម្រុយ៖ ជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ទាក់ទាញចំណាប់អារម្មណ៍ និងមានមហិច្ឆតា។
5. Symbol

## Voice-over – 180-second video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Born in the Year of the Dragon? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, Monday, 2 November 2026, is a Dragon day. It is a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Dragon. This is your day to shine!

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Ruler of the clouds and rain, the Dragon symbolizes power, good fortune, and a noble spirit. Today, this good energy is with you.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
People born in the Year of the Dragon are confident, charismatic, and ambitious. Dragons draw attention and often rise to leadership naturally.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Born in the Year of the Dragon? Your luck is strong today. Stay majestic, proud and noble. Say yes to good chances, and share your happiness with the people around you.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Love and family today: A good day to reach out, share a meal and show your affection openly.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Work today: Customers and coworkers respond well to you. A good day to present your ideas and close a deal.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Money today: Good for fair deals and collecting what is owed. Keep your records neat.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
Your lucky color today is gold. Wear something gold, or carry a small gold item with you, to keep good luck close.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
Your best time today is from 3 to 5 in the afternoon. It is a good time for important plans, meetings, or a fresh start.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Your lucky direction today is the southwest. When you start something new, face the southwest, and walk forward with a calm and happy heart.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
For lucky numbers: 1, 75, and 147. Comment your lucky number below, and let's say it again: 1, 75, 147!

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
The Dragon's lucky days of the week are Tuesday and Saturday. Keep them in mind for plans that matter to you.

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
A gentle reminder: people born in the Year of the Dragon can come across as proud or impatient with limitations. Today, stay balanced, and listen to your heart.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
Make a small wish today, and take one kind step toward it. A peaceful heart helps good luck find its way to you.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
Before the day ends, take a quiet moment to say thank you. Thank the people who help you, and thank yourself for trying your best.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: gold. Best time: from 3 to 5 in the afternoon. Lucky direction: southwest. Lucky numbers: 1, 75, and 147.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

```
5
00:00:12,578 --> 00:00:14,716

6

7

8

10

11

12

13

14

15

16

17
00:00:42,222 --> 00:00:44,172

18

19
00:00:46,889 --> 00:00:49,950

20

21
00:00:52,500 --> 00:00:56,950
ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់ទាក់ទងគ្នា ញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា

22

23

24

25

30

31

32

ជាប់ខ្លួន ដើម្បីរក្សាសំណាងល្អ ឲ្យនៅជិតអ្នក។

34

35

39

52

ហើយធ្វើរឿងល្អមួយ ដើម្បីឈានទៅរកក្ដីប្រាថ្នានោះ។

54

58

61
00:02:42,992 --> 00:02:45,777

62

63
00:02:47,323 --> 00:02:49,950

64

65
00:02:52,308 --> 00:02:54,373

00:02:54,423 --> 00:02:56,232

67
```

## Long YouTube video – all 12 signs (about 5 minutes)
A long YouTube video (5:10): today's luckiest animal, an overview, then all 12 signs in order (two 10-second clips each), then a short closing. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs. Add Chanda and the 12 saved characters as ingredients (@Rat, @Ox … @Pig; each sign's clips name its own character). Chanda opens the video on camera and narrates the rest off screen; the animals never speak. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 31.

### YouTube chapters (paste into the description)
0:00 Introduction
0:40 Rat (ជូត) – Good Day
1:00 Ox (ឆ្លូវ) – Ordinary Day
1:20 Tiger (ខាល) – Ordinary Day
1:40 Rabbit (ថោះ) – Ordinary Day
2:00 Dragon (រោង) – Great Day
2:20 Snake (ម្សាញ់) – Ordinary Day
2:40 Horse (មមី) – Ordinary Day
3:00 Goat (មមែ) – Ordinary Day
3:20 Monkey (វក) – Good Day
3:40 Rooster (រកា) – Ordinary Day
4:00 Dog (ច) – Take It Easy
4:20 Pig (កុរ) – Ordinary Day
4:40 Closing

### Topic and audience
A full daily fortune for all twelve Chinese zodiac signs. For anyone who wants their own sign's rating, lucky color, best time and direction; chapters let each viewer jump to their sign.

### Thumbnail prompt – YouTube (16:9)
Make the picture in an image AI (for example Gemini or Flow) with Chanda's picture and the saved zodiac characters (named after their animals) added as references. Upload size: 16:9, at least 640 pixels wide (3840 x 2160 is best), JPG or PNG, under 2 MB if you upload from your phone.

With the words drawn in (English):
Eye-catching YouTube thumbnail, horizontal 16:9, 3840 x 2160. Photorealistic still from a high-budget fantasy epic blending Khmer royal heritage with a Chinese historical fantasy drama: real human faces with natural skin, lifelike zodiac animals in engraved ceremonial gold armor with real metallic reflections, very expressive faces and eyes. Background: a glowing Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat under an enormous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky, lotus pool, golden particles. Bright, high-contrast, rich colors (imperial gold, deep navy, ruby red) that stand out on a small phone screen; strong golden rim light separating the characters from the background; slightly blurred background so the faces pop. Big, simple composition with only two or three subjects. Keep the bottom-right corner free (YouTube puts the video length there). No watermark, no logo, no YouTube buttons or arrows, no cartoon or plastic look. The twelve golden-armored zodiac guardians in a tight arc across the frame, all looking at the camera with excited faces; three of them shine much brighter with golden auras, one rests calmly in a soft blue bubble of light. Large bold text, at most a few words, in thick rounded sans-serif capitals with a dark outline and a soft drop shadow, perfectly spelled: "ALL 12 SIGNS" and "TODAY". The text sits across the top of the frame in the dark sky, never over a face.

### Audio design
Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. No other voices: the animals do not speak. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. Sound effects, used lightly: a soft chime when a lucky fact is revealed, a gentle whoosh on scene changes, a warm swell at the ending. No loud or scary sounds.

### Subtitles – English (2026-11-02-long.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:03,243

2

3

4
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:12,206
Today, Monday, 2 November 2026

5
00:00:12,256 --> 00:00:13,409
is a Dragon day.

6
00:00:13,459 --> 00:00:16,040
It is a Great Day for everyone born

7
00:00:16,090 --> 00:00:17,995
in the Year of the Dragon.

8

9

11

12

14

15

16

18

22
00:00:53,143 --> 00:00:54,902
with someone close.

23

24

25
```

## Voice-over – story video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Can one little golden dragon wake the night flowers before the full moon rises? Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple in Cambodia, our story begins.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
But this morning, the flowers fell into a deep sleep. Nothing could wake them. Without their glow, the festival could not begin.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus opened, and out came a little golden dragon. Today was a Dragon day, and the dragon felt something special.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
A little frog named Kong came hopping over. "Little Dragon," he said, "only you can wake the night flowers. Today is your lucky day!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Ruler of the clouds and rain, the Dragon symbolizes power, good fortune, and a noble spirit. Kong smiled. That was exactly why the moon had chosen it.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
To wake the night flowers, the little hero needed three lucky treasures: its lucky color, its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
"Let's go!" said the dragon, and it set off on its adventure, with Kong right by its side.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
People born in the Year of the Dragon are confident, charismatic, and ambitious. Dragons draw attention and often rise to leadership naturally.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
The first treasure was waiting in a garden of a thousand lanterns. Only one shone brightest. It was gold, the dragon's lucky color today.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
The dragon tied a gold ribbon around its neck. "Wear your lucky color," said Kong, "and good luck stays close to you."

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Next, they came to a crossroads with many paths. One path began to glow. It pointed to the southwest, the dragon's lucky direction.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
But the path climbed a steep stone hill, with a strong wind blowing. The dragon stopped. Sometimes, people born in the Year of the Dragon can come across as proud or impatient with limitations.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Kong came close and smiled. "Breathe slowly," he said. "Trust your heart. You are stronger than you think."

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Step by step, the dragon climbed the hill. The wind grew softer, and at the top, it laughed with joy.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The little hero learned that its worries were small, and its good heart was big. Balance and courage always help us grow.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
At last, they reached a great stone door in the temple. Carved above it were bells, stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
"The door opens only at your best time," said Kong. For the Dragon today, that is from 3 to 5 in the afternoon. They waited patiently.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
When the time came, the dragon rang the golden bells once, its first lucky number. Ding! The door began to shine.

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
The door opened, and a stairway of light appeared. The dragon climbed 75 glowing steps, its second lucky number, up to the top of the tower.

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
At the top of the tower, they found a single drop of golden dew, sparkling in a crystal shell. It was the magic that wakes the flowers!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
Around the dew drop, seven little lights twinkled, one for each day of the week. Tuesday and Saturday shone brightest, the Dragon's lucky days.

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
Time was running out. Holding the dew drop carefully, the little hero hurried home, with Kong right beside it.

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting. The night was dark, and the full moon was rising. Was it too late?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
The dragon let the golden dew fall on the flowers. For a moment, nothing happened. Then, one by one, the flowers opened and glowed!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
The night flowers glowed again. Lanterns rose into the sky, and the full-moon festival began. Everyone cheered for the little golden hero!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
The full moon shone down warmly, as if to say thank you. The dragon felt peaceful and proud. It had done its very best.

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
If you were born in the Year of the Dragon, today is your day too. Like our hero, be brave, be kind, and follow your good heart.

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)

```
"

"

អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរោង ជួនកាលមើលទៅអំនួត

50

ឬអត់ធ្មត់ទាបចំពោះដែនកំណត់។

51

គង់មកជិត ហើយញញឹម។

52
00:02:01,700 --> 00:02:04,150
គង់និយាយថា "ដកដង្ហើមយឺតៗ។

53

ជឿលើចិត្តរបស់អ្នក។

54

"

56

នៅក្នុងប្រាសាទ។

64

68

76

77

78

នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ

79
00:03:11,455 --> 00:03:14,677

80
00:03:14,727 --> 00:03:17,586

81

82

84

85

86
00:03:28,200 --> 00:03:29,950

87

ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។

88
00:03:31,589 --> 00:03:35,838

89
00:03:35,888 --> 00:03:39,950

90
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:44,485

91
00:03:44,535 --> 00:03:48,438

92
00:03:48,488 --> 00:03:49,950
តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

93
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:53,027
នាគទម្លាក់ទឹកសន្សើមមាស ទៅលើផ្កា។

94
00:03:53,077 --> 00:03:55,719

95

96
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:01,897

97
00:04:01,947 --> 00:04:03,136

98
00:04:03,186 --> 00:04:06,587

99
00:04:06,637 --> 00:04:09,950

100
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:13,147

101
00:04:13,197 --> 00:04:15,032
ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។

102

103
00:04:17,869 --> 00:04:19,950

104
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,916

105
00:04:23,966 --> 00:04:26,588

106

107
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,604
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

108
00:04:31,654 --> 00:04:32,942

109
00:04:32,992 --> 00:04:35,777

110
00:04:35,827 --> 00:04:37,273
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសនិរតី។

111
00:04:37,323 --> 00:04:39,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 1 លេខ 75 និងលេខ 147។

112
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:44,155
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់

113
00:04:44,205 --> 00:04:47,450
វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងគង់ ក៏លង់លក់

114
00:04:47,500 --> 00:04:49,950
ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

115
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

116
00:04:52,308 --> 00:04:54,373

00:04:54,423 --> 00:04:56,232
```

## Take-it-easy video – the unlucky sign, Dog (8 scenes, 80 seconds)
An 80-second video for the Dog, today's sign to take it easy (in the traditional day cycle the Dog clashes with the Dragon day): 8 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add Chanda and the saved character @Dog as ingredients (the Dog is the only zodiac character in this video, from the first scene to the last). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1 and 8; in the other scenes she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. It is one continuous little story in one place (the dog hurries, wobbles, stops to breathe, a little floating candle keeps it company, it climbs the temple steps and looks out with hope), so make the clips in order and keep the same setting and light. No lucky numbers or colors in this video: it is about encouragement. Keep it gentle and realistic: a quiet day, not bad luck. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 8.

## Voice-over – take-it-easy video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Stop scrolling! Born in the Year of the Dog? Your luck isn't good today. But don't worry, watch to the end.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, 2 November, is a Dragon day, opposite the Dog in the zodiac circle. Some days don't go to plan.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
And that's okay. Everyone has days like this. A quiet day is not a bad day. It is a day to breathe.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Be careful today: you can be anxious or overly suspicious of new situations. Pause before you react. Small steps still count.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Be gentle with the people you love, and with yourself. And lean on a friend you trust.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
At work and with money, take small, careful steps. Finish one thing, then the next. Take a deep breath first.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Slow is still moving forward. Every quiet day makes you stronger, and brighter days are already on their way.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Rest well tonight. Tomorrow is a new day, and it could be yours. Follow MyBirthSign for your fortune every day!

## Long YouTube video – full fortune, 8:40 (mid-roll ads)
A full daily fortune for YouTube (8:40, 52 clips of 10 seconds), long enough for mid-roll ads. In order: the hook, the Great Day sign (Dragon) in depth, the two Good Day signs (Rat and Monkey), the sign to take it easy (Dog) with its little story, the other eight signs, then a recap and goodbye. Add Chanda and the 12 saved characters as ingredients in Flow (@Rat, @Ox … @Pig; each part names its own character). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1, 4 and 52; in the others she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. Set the clip length to 10 seconds, make the clips in order 1 to 52, and paste the chapters into the YouTube description.

### YouTube chapters (paste into the description)
0:00 Introduction
0:40 Great Day: Dragon
2:40 Good Day: Rat
3:30 Good Day: Monkey
4:20 Take it easy: Dog
5:20 All the other signs
8:00 Recap and goodbye

### Opening hook – 5 options
1. Every sign in one line
   English: One sign has a Great Day today, two have a Good Day, and one should slow down. Which one are you?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ មានមួយឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ពីរឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អ និងមួយឆ្នាំគួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ។ តើអ្នកជាឆ្នាំណា?
2. Before a big decision
   English: Before you make any big decision today, watch this: your lucky color, your best time, and whether to slow down.
   Khmer: មុននឹងសម្រេចចិត្តរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ៖ ពណ៌សំណាង ពេលល្អ និងថាតើគួរធ្វើយឺតៗឬទេ។
★ 3. Speak to the careful sign
   English: Born in the Year of the Dog? Watch this before you do anything big today. Everyone else, your luck is here too.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំចមែនទេ? សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ មុនធ្វើរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកផ្សេងទៀត សំណាងរបស់អ្នកក៏នៅទីនេះដែរ។
4. Name the winner
   English: Today the Dragon has its Great Day, and one sign should take it easy. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ ឆ្នាំរោងមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ហើយមានមួយឆ្នាំគួរប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។ តើជាឆ្នាំរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
5. Comment first
   English: Comment your sign now. By the end of this video, you'll know your lucky color, best time, and direction for today.
   Khmer: សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នកឥឡូវនេះ។ មុនវីដេអូនេះចប់ អ្នកនឹងដឹងពណ៌ ពេល និងទិសសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។
★ = used to open this video today (the hook changes from day to day, and each one gives every viewer a reason to stay to find their own sign). To use another hook, replace the voice line in scene 1.

### Mid-roll ads
YouTube lets you add ad breaks in the middle of videos that are 8 minutes or longer; this one is 8:40. Put the breaks at chapter starts so they don't cut a sentence. Chapter starts after 2:00: 2:40, 3:30, 4:20, 5:20, 8:00. Two or three breaks are enough; too many make viewers leave.

Make the picture in an image AI (for example Gemini or Flow) with Chanda's picture and the saved zodiac characters (named after their animals) added as references. Upload size: 16:9, at least 640 pixels wide (3840 x 2160 is best), JPG or PNG, under 2 MB if you upload from your phone.

### Scene 1 – The hook – Chanda on camera (0:00–0:10)
@Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands on the moonlit temple terrace beside the lotus pond, facing the camera, smiling warmly and speaking directly to the viewer with natural lip movements and small, friendly hand gestures. The animals move gently and look around curiously, mouths closed. Medium shot, gentle push-in toward her face. ## Voice-over – full fortune video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Born in the Year of the Dog? Watch this before you do anything big today. Everyone else, your luck is here too.

Today is a Dragon day. Coming up: one Great Day sign, two Good Days, one sign to take it easy, and all twelve.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Ruler of the clouds and rain, the Dragon symbolizes power, good fortune, and a noble spirit. Let that spirit guide you today.

```
00:00:18,529 --> 00:00:19,950
និងរាសីទាំង 12។

8
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:22,190
ឆ្នាំរោង ឆ្នាំជូត និងឆ្នាំវក

9
00:00:22,240 --> 00:00:25,630
មានសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ ហើយឆ្នាំច គួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ។

10
00:00:25,680 --> 00:00:28,590
សូមរករាសីរបស់អ្នក ក្នុងជំពូកខាងក្រោម។

11
00:00:28,640 --> 00:00:29,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

12
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:33,136
មុននឹងចាប់ផ្ដើម សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នក

13
00:00:33,186 --> 00:00:35,525
ក្នុងមតិយោបល់ ហើយ subscribe

14
00:00:35,575 --> 00:00:38,003
ដើម្បីទទួលជោគជតារៀងរាល់ថ្ងៃ។

15
00:00:38,053 --> 00:00:39,950
ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

16
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,586
ថ្ងៃច័ន្ទ ថ្ងៃទី 2 ខែវិច្ឆិកា

17
00:00:42,636 --> 00:00:46,132
ជាថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង សម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរោង!

18
00:00:46,182 --> 00:00:49,950
តោះ មកមើលថា ថ្ងៃសំណាងនេះ នាំអ្វីមកឲ្យអ្នក។

19
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,539
នាគ គឺជាម្ចាស់នៃពពក និងភ្លៀង។

20
00:00:52,589 --> 00:00:54,414
ឆ្នាំរោងតំណាងឲ្យអំណាច

21
00:00:54,464 --> 00:00:56,825
សំណាងល្អ និងចិត្តថ្លៃថ្នូរ។

22
00:00:56,875 --> 00:00:59,950
សូមឲ្យស្មារតីនោះ ដឹកនាំអ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ។

23
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:01,914
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរោង ជារឿយៗ

24
00:01:01,964 --> 00:01:04,950
ត្រូវបានគេស្គាល់ថា ជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង

25
00:01:05,000 --> 00:01:08,254
ទាក់ទាញចំណាប់អារម្មណ៍ និងមានមហិច្ឆតា។

26
00:01:08,304 --> 00:01:09,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

27
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:14,117
ថ្ងៃនេះ គឺជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នក។

28
00:01:14,167 --> 00:01:19,950
ជម្រើសល្អៗតូចៗ នាំមកនូវថ្ងៃដ៏សំណាង។

29
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,087
ស្នេហា និងគ្រួសារថ្ងៃនេះ៖

30
00:01:22,137 --> 00:01:25,933
ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់ទាក់ទងគ្នា ញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា

31
00:01:25,983 --> 00:01:28,497
និងបង្ហាញក្ដីស្រឡាញ់ដោយបើកចំហ។

32
00:01:28,547 --> 00:01:29,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

33
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:31,987
ការងារថ្ងៃនេះ៖ អតិថិជន

34
00:01:32,037 --> 00:01:35,506
និងមិត្តរួមការងារ ឆ្លើយតបល្អចំពោះអ្នក។

35
00:01:35,556 --> 00:01:38,006
ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់បង្ហាញគំនិត

36
00:01:38,056 --> 00:01:39,950
និងបិទកិច្ចព្រមព្រៀង។

37
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:41,211
លុយកាក់ថ្ងៃនេះ៖

38
00:01:41,261 --> 00:01:43,984
ល្អសម្រាប់កិច្ចព្រមព្រៀងយុត្តិធម៌

39
00:01:44,034 --> 00:01:46,253
និងប្រមូលប្រាក់ដែលគេជំពាក់។

40
00:01:46,303 --> 00:01:48,353
សូមរក្សាបញ្ជីឲ្យបានស្អាត។

41
00:01:48,403 --> 00:01:49,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

42
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:52,162
ពណ៌សំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ គឺពណ៌មាស។

43
00:01:52,212 --> 00:01:56,145
កន្សែង កាបូប ឬខ្សែដៃពណ៌មាស នាំសំណាងមកជិតអ្នក។

44
00:01:56,195 --> 00:01:59,950
សូមឲ្យវារំលឹកអ្នក ឲ្យញញឹម និងមានទំនុកចិត្ត។

45
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:02,807
ពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោង 3 រសៀល

46
00:02:02,857 --> 00:02:07,450
ដល់ម៉ោង 5 ល្ងាច ហើយទិសសំណាង គឺទិសនិរតី។

47
00:02:07,500 --> 00:02:09,950
សូមរៀបចំផែនការតាមនោះ។

48
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:12,899
លេខសំណាង៖ 1 75 និង 147។

49
00:02:12,949 --> 00:02:17,258
សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នក ក្នុងមតិយោបល់

50
00:02:17,308 --> 00:02:19,950
ហើយរក្សាទុកវីដេអូនេះ!

51
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:22,233
ថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំរោង

52
00:02:22,283 --> 00:02:24,950
គឺថ្ងៃអង្គារ និងថ្ងៃសៅរ៍។

53
00:02:25,000 --> 00:02:27,885
សូមទុកសម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ។

54
00:02:27,935 --> 00:02:29,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

55
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,495
រំលឹកបន្តិច៖ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរោង

56
00:02:32,545 --> 00:02:36,405
ជួនកាលមើលទៅអំនួត ឬអត់ធ្មត់ទាបចំពោះដែនកំណត់។

57
00:02:36,455 --> 00:02:37,950
សូមរក្សាតុល្យភាព។

58
00:02:38,000 --> 00:02:39,950
ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

59
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:42,871
បន្ទាប់ ឆ្នាំជូត៖ ថ្ងៃល្អ!

60
00:02:42,921 --> 00:02:48,040
ឆ្នាំជូត ស្ថិតក្នុងត្រីកោណសំណាង ជាមួយឆ្នាំរោង។

61
00:02:48,090 --> 00:02:49,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

62
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:52,382
ថ្ងៃនេះ ល្អសម្រាប់ជួបមនុស្ស

63
00:02:52,432 --> 00:02:54,184
និងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងល្អៗ។

64
00:02:54,234 --> 00:02:55,265
ស្នេហា៖

65
00:02:55,315 --> 00:02:58,779
សូមចំណាយពេលបន្តិចជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ។

66
00:02:58,829 --> 00:02:59,950
ថ្ងៃធាតុលោហៈ!

67
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:02,075
ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌បៃតង

68
00:03:02,125 --> 00:03:07,575
ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោង 3 រសៀល ដល់ម៉ោង 5 ល្ងាច។

69
00:03:07,625 --> 00:03:09,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

70
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:11,993
ទិសសំណាងគឺទិសនិរតី។

71
00:03:12,043 --> 00:03:14,466
លេខសំណាង៖ 3 23 និង 363។

72
00:03:14,516 --> 00:03:15,756
សូមញញឹម

73
00:03:15,806 --> 00:03:19,950
ហើយចែករំលែកសំណាងជាមួយនរណាម្នាក់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

74
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,423
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំជូត ថ្ងៃនេះ

75
00:03:22,473 --> 00:03:25,004
សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរោង។

76
00:03:25,054 --> 00:03:28,122
សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។

77
00:03:28,172 --> 00:03:29,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

78
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:32,962
បន្ទាប់ ឆ្នាំវក៖ ថ្ងៃល្អ!

79
00:03:33,012 --> 00:03:38,384
ឆ្នាំវក ស្ថិតក្នុងត្រីកោណសំណាង ជាមួយឆ្នាំរោង។

80
00:03:38,434 --> 00:03:39,950
ថ្ងៃធាតុលោហៈ!

81
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:42,258
ថ្ងៃនេះ ល្អសម្រាប់ជួបមនុស្ស

82
00:03:42,308 --> 00:03:43,967
និងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងល្អៗ។

83
00:03:44,017 --> 00:03:44,993
ស្នេហា៖

84
00:03:45,043 --> 00:03:48,326
សូមចំណាយពេលបន្តិចជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ។

85
00:03:48,376 --> 00:03:49,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

86
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:52,028
ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌មាស

87
00:03:52,078 --> 00:03:57,093
ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោង 7 ដល់ម៉ោង 9 ព្រឹក។

88
00:03:57,143 --> 00:03:59,950
ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

89
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,242
ទិសសំណាងគឺទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។

90
00:04:02,292 --> 00:04:04,638
លេខសំណាង៖ 9 42 និង 443។

91
00:04:04,688 --> 00:04:05,888
សូមញញឹម

92
00:04:05,938 --> 00:04:09,950
ហើយចែករំលែកសំណាងជាមួយនរណាម្នាក់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

93
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:12,450
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំវក ថ្ងៃនេះ

94
00:04:12,500 --> 00:04:15,177
សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរោង។

95
00:04:15,227 --> 00:04:18,473
សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។

96
00:04:18,523 --> 00:04:19,950
ថ្ងៃធាតុលោហៈ!

97
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,839
ថ្ងៃនេះ ថ្ងៃទី 2 ខែវិច្ឆិកា ជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំរោង

98
00:04:23,889 --> 00:04:26,524
ទល់មុខឆ្នាំច ក្នុងរង្វង់រាសី។

99
00:04:26,574 --> 00:04:29,950
មានថ្ងៃខ្លះ អ្វីៗមិនដូចការគ្រោងទុកទេ។

100
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,090
ហើយវាមិនអីទេ។

101
00:04:31,140 --> 00:04:34,248
មនុស្សគ្រប់គ្នា ធ្លាប់មានថ្ងៃបែបនេះ។

102
00:04:34,298 --> 00:04:37,494
ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ មិនមែនជាថ្ងៃអាក្រក់ទេ

103
00:04:37,544 --> 00:04:39,950
គឺជាថ្ងៃសម្រាប់ដកដង្ហើមវែងៗ។

104
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:43,283
សូមប្រយ័ត្នថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជួនកាលព្រួយបារម្ភ

105
00:04:43,333 --> 00:04:45,355
ឬសង្ស័យស្ថានភាពថ្មីពេក។

106
00:04:45,405 --> 00:04:47,788
សូមឈប់បន្តិច មុននឹងឆ្លើយតប។

107
00:04:47,838 --> 00:04:49,950
ជំហានតូចៗ ក៏មានតម្លៃដែរ។

108
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:54,394
សូមទន់ភ្លន់ចំពោះមនុស្សដែលអ្នកស្រឡាញ់

109
00:04:54,444 --> 00:04:56,370
និងចំពោះខ្លួនឯង។

110
00:04:56,420 --> 00:04:59,950
ហើយពឹងលើមិត្តដែលអ្នកទុកចិត្ត។

111
00:05:00,000 --> 00:05:01,897
ក្នុងការងារ និងលុយកាក់

112
00:05:01,947 --> 00:05:04,817
សូមបោះជំហានតូចៗ ដោយប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។

113
00:05:04,867 --> 00:05:07,826
បញ្ចប់រឿងមួយ រួចសឹមធ្វើរឿងបន្ទាប់។

114
00:05:07,876 --> 00:05:09,950
សូមដកដង្ហើមវែងៗជាមុនសិន។

115
00:05:10,000 --> 00:05:12,705
យឺត ក៏នៅតែជាការឈានទៅមុខដែរ។

116
00:05:12,755 --> 00:05:17,093
```

### Subtitles – English (2026-11-02-yt8.en.srt)

```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,543
Born in the Year of the Dog?

2
00:00:02,593 --> 00:00:04,487
Watch this before you

3
00:00:04,537 --> 00:00:06,524

4
00:00:06,574 --> 00:00:09,950

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:12,025

6

7

8
00:00:16,981 --> 00:00:19,950

9

10
00:00:23,167 --> 00:00:25,533

11

12

13

14

15

16

17

18
00:00:41,652 --> 00:00:44,124

19
00:00:44,174 --> 00:00:46,385

20
00:00:46,435 --> 00:00:49,950

21

22

23
00:00:54,583 --> 00:00:57,283

24
00:00:57,333 --> 00:00:59,950

25

People born in the Year

26

27
00:01:04,870 --> 00:01:08,385

28

Keep a calm heart.

29

30

31

32

33

34

Share your luck.

35

36

37

38
00:01:38,365 --> 00:01:39,950

39

40
00:01:43,168 --> 00:01:45,891

41
00:01:45,941 --> 00:01:48,168
Keep your records neat.

42
00:01:48,218 --> 00:01:49,950
Keep a calm heart.

43

44
00:01:52,393 --> 00:01:53,796

45
00:01:53,846 --> 00:01:56,104

46

47

48

Your best time is
```

## Keep the voice the same in every scene
Flow makes the voice fresh for every clip, so the voice text in every scene is written to describe exactly the same voice: the same words, in the same order, at the start of the audio line, for every scene and every video. To keep it that way:
- Always use "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Do not retype, shorten or translate the voice text, and do not add other words about the voice.
- Make all clips of one video in the same Flow project, with the same model and settings for every clip.
- Use the same language for the whole video (all English or all Khmer).
- Listen to each clip as it is made. If one sounds different, make only that clip again with the same copied prompt.

## Khmer voice – how to get clear Khmer
Every Khmer voice line in this pack is written for speaking: short sentences, a ។ at each pause, and numbers written out in Khmer words (for example ហុកសិបបួន, not 64) so the voice does not read them in English. Subtitles and captions keep the digits.

### If you use Google Flow
Use "Copy + Khmer voice" for each scene; the voice description is already included. Flow's Khmer can be unclear on some clips. Listen to every clip, and remake any clip where words are wrong, cut off or English-sounding. If Khmer stays unclear, make the clips without voice ("Copy") and add a recorded Khmer voice-over instead.

### If you use a text-to-speech app
- Language: Cambodian Khmer (km-KH). Pick a real Khmer voice, for example Microsoft Azure's female voice "km-KH-SreymomNeural" (used by many TTS apps and sites). Do not use an English voice with Khmer text.
- Speed: 0.85× to 0.9×. Pitch: natural. Pause between sentences: normal.
- Paste the Khmer voice-over text from this pack exactly as it is (Khmer script, no romanization).
- Make one scene at a time if the app cuts off long text, then join the clips in your editor.
- Before using a new voice, test it with these two sentences and listen carefully:
  សួស្តីអ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា! សូមស្វាគមន៍មកកាន់ MyBirthSign.com។
  ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខ្លា។ សូមជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ហើយកុំភ្លេចអត់ធ្មត់។
  Check that every word is clear Cambodian Khmer, sentence endings are complete, and speed and volume stay even.

### Record it yourself
A native speaker reading the Khmer voice-over slowly and clearly is always the most natural choice.

## Style (paste first in every scene)
Vertical 9:16. Photorealistic live-action fantasy film, the look of a high-budget fantasy epic that blends Khmer royal heritage with the grandeur of a Chinese historical fantasy drama. Real human faces with natural skin texture, the zodiac characters saved in Flow, each named after its animal (@Rat, @Ox, @Tiger, @Rabbit, @Dragon, @Snake, @Horse, @Goat, @Monkey, @Rooster, @Dog, @Pig): a guardian warrior in ornate armor themed on the animal, with a lifelike animal beside them (realistic anatomy, real fur, feathers or scales), always exactly as in the saved character picture, noble and powerful (never cute toys or cartoons). Setting: a majestic Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat with towering carved stone, an enormous luminous full moon, deep indigo starry sky, drifting mist, a still reflecting pool with floating pink lotus flowers and ornate golden lanterns. A living, magical sky in every shot: thin silver clouds drift slowly across the moon, stars twinkle, a soft halo pulses around the moon and sky lanterns rise gently in the distance. Action and magic in the style of a Chinese fantasy (xianxia) drama: graceful wire-fu flying leaps, slow-motion martial-arts moves, flowing robes and capes, glowing sword energy and swirling qi light, always without blood, injury or fighting between characters. Golden magic: shimmering gold particles, flowing ribbons of liquid-gold light and floating gold dust that never hide a face. Physically real lighting: cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light, soft lantern glow, volumetric light rays; colors deep navy, imperial gold, ruby red and lotus pink. Cinematic depth of field, smooth slow camera movement, high-end visual effects, 4K detail. The zodiac guardians and their animals never talk and never move their mouths; only Chanda, the presenter, speaks. No text, no letters, no subtitles, no watermark, no logos, no cartoon or plastic look, no low-quality CGI.

## Scene 1 – Dragon day (Chanda on camera)
@Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands in front of the Angkor-style temple at night, facing the camera and speaking to the viewer with natural lip movements, then turns and gestures toward the pond. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda. Then: magical and powerful. @Dragon (the Dragon guardian with their dragon) stands on its glowing lotus pedestal on the pond in front of the Angkor-style temple under a huge full moon. Its eyes light up, a golden aura bursts around it, and it chases a glowing pearl around the temple towers while its guardian leaps from tower to tower in giant weightless bounds, catching the pearl in mid-air, as today's Metal magic swirls around them: bright silver-white sparkles, shimmering blades of light and softly chiming silver stars. The lotus pond ripples with golden light, and rings of light spread across the pond. Dynamic camera: start close on its face, then sweep up and follow it through the air. Cute, majestic and full of energy, matching the reference image.

## Scene 2 – Lucky signs
Magical and powerful. @Dragon (the Dragon guardian with their dragon), @Rat (the Rat guardian with their rat) and @Monkey (the Monkey guardian with their monkey) fly in from three directions on trails of golden light and land together on glowing lotus pedestals in front of the temple. As they land, bells ring out from the temple towers and rings of golden light ripple across the sky. They look at each other and cheer. The camera orbits around them in a smooth circle. The animals match the reference images.

## Scene 3 – Dog, take it easy
Photorealistic golden fantasy-epic style, same saved Flow characters (Chanda and the zodiac characters named after their animals). @Dog (the Dog guardian with their dog) rests on a lotus pedestal by the pond: it naps peacefully under a big lotus leaf held up like an umbrella, and the full moon shines calmly on the still water. The mood is peaceful, safe and magical, not sad. The camera moves in slowly. The dog is the saved Flow character @Dog.

## Scene 4 – Outro
Magical and powerful. Thousands of fireflies rise from the pond and gather into a huge glowing lotus flower in the sky, and the huge full moon glows over the pond. The camera rises up and pulls back for a wide, epic view, leaving open sky at the top of the frame for a title.

## Scene 5 – Lucky numbers (about 10–12 seconds)
Magical and powerful. A huge glowing golden zodiac wheel rises out of the lotus pond in front of the temple, with the twelve golden-armored zodiac guardians around its rim. It spins faster and faster, shooting beams of golden light and sparkles, then slows to a gentle, shining turn under the full moon. The camera slowly pushes in, keeping open space in the middle and lower part of the frame for the number captions. (Loop the slow-turning part, or slow-zoom a still picture, to fill the time.)
